# Source Library catalogue (the second book store)

Generated by `scripts/library_catalogue.py` from `~/.local/share/source-platform/catalogue.sqlite`, read-only: 3500 assets. Titles, authors and kinds only. Files live at `~/.local/share/source-platform/library/<sha256>/` (the short hash below is its first 10 characters). Many books here were also imported into Calibre; the same title can appear in both, and some titles repeat here under several editions. Mostly tabletop rulebooks and light novels. Use the Source Library CLI or MCP to search and download; see [source acquisition tools](SOURCE_ACQUISITION_TOOLS.md). Prime books: [LIBRARY_INDEX](LIBRARY_INDEX.md).
